To achieve robust and accurate tracking, this paper concentrates on the event-based multi-dimensional assignment that associates tracklets across multiple batches simultaneously. Unlike image-based detectors, event-based detectors can generate multiple correct yet spatially distinct tracklets for a single object per batch. This violates the one-to-one rule in classic data association, making event-based association an underdetermined many-to-many matching problem. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to tackle this NP-hard problem. To address its underdetermined nature, we formulate the assignment problem via fuzzy logic, where pairwise tracklet correlations are quantified through multiple long-range kinematic metrics. This fuzzy design eliminates the need for hard tracking thresholds and enables multiple heterogeneous metrics to be integrated into a unified correlation. By treating tracklets as nodes and their fuzzy correlations as edges, we construct a Hierarchical Sparse Graph (HSG). Building upon the HSG, we pose assignment as a graph partition task, and employ a heuristic solution that measures vertex affinity for partitioning adaptively. The entire pipeline is training-free. Comprehensive experiments on two challenging event datasets, eTram and Ev-UAV, show that our method outperforms existing event-based and most image-based association models. Particularly, it achieves over 2$\times$ the performance of OC-SORT across all metrics in the many-to-many association scenario.

</details>


### [106] [ScIntBench: Physical Interaction Plausibility in 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37326)
*Wendi Ying,Daochang Liu,Leonid Sigal,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScIntBench基准，以“交互就绪性”为核心评估文本条件3D室内场景生成，通过物理仿真执行推、开、表面探测等操作，度量任务成功率、能力与进度、交互后完整性和动态合理性。评测12个SOTA系统覆盖10类房间，发现语义与静态物理表现强并不预示交互就绪：静态合理性近乎完美的系统交互完成率仅11.54%，12个系统中7个无法支撑任何交互，最佳系统成功率仅36.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件3D室内场景生成的评测主要聚焦静态语义保真度与物理有效性，对交互过程中的物理可靠性评估不足，缺少衡量“交互就绪性”的统一基准。

Method: 构建ScIntBench统一基准：在物理仿真中执行预设操作动作（推、开、表面探测），度量任务成功率、能力与进度、交互后完整性、动态合理性；并以多视角语义评估与静态物理评估作为补充，用于解释交互结果。系统评测12个先进场景生成系统、10类房间。

Result: 静态物理近乎完美的系统交互完成率仅11.54%；12个系统中7个生成的场景无法支撑任何交互；最佳系统在预设任务上成功率仅36.92%，表明语义与静态物理性能强并不必然转化为交互就绪性。

Conclusion: 生成交互就绪场景面临两大根本挑战：提供必要的物理交互能力，以及保证主动操作下可靠的物理行为。静态指标不足以表征交互可靠性，需引入交互驱动的动态评估。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation largely emphasize semantic fidelity and physical validity at rest, leaving interaction-time physical reliability insufficiently assessed. To address this gap, we introduce ScIntBench, a unified benchmark centered on interaction readiness: it executes prescribed manipulation actions (e.g., pushing, opening, and surface probing) in physics simulation and measures task success, capability and progress, post-interaction integrity, and dynamic plausibility. Multi-view semantic and static physical evaluations complement these metrics and help explain interaction outcomes. We systematically evaluate 12 state-of-the-art scene generation systems across 10 room types. Our results reveal that strong semantic and static physical performance does not necessarily translate into interaction readiness: a system with near-perfect static plausibility completes only 11.54% of interactions, 7 of the 12 systems produce scenes that cannot support any interaction, and even the best system succeeds in only 36.92% of prescribed tasks. These findings highlight two fundamental challenges in generating interaction-ready scenes: providing the necessary physical interaction capabilities and ensuring reliable physical behavior under active manipulation.

</details>


### [107] [CodeCap-Bench: Can Code Serve as a Good Video Caption?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37341)
*Jiafu Tang,Yifan Xu,Eric Tang,Junjie Zhao,Shipeng Luo,Te Sun,Haoxuan Hu,Yiyan Ji,Xinlong Chen,Zihao Wang,Yang Shi,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"代码即视频字幕"(Code as Video Caption)范式，用可执行程序（图形基元、布局规则、时间函数）作为自然语言的互补视频表示，并构建 CodeCap-Bench 评测 MLLM 生成忠实代码字幕的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言描述细粒度空间结构与时序动态时冗长且易歧义，难以精确保留视频中的视觉信息，因此需要更精确、紧凑的替代/补充表示形式。

Method: 以可执行代码作为视频字幕，在问答与视频重建两个下游任务上与自然语言字幕对比；并构建 CodeCap-Bench（323 个视频、五类结构化 2D 内容、细粒度标注与多级评价框架），系统评估当前 MLLM 生成代码字幕的忠实度。

Result: 代码字幕在推理准确率与重建保真度上优于自然语言字幕；实验显示领先的闭源 MLLM 已展现可观的代码字幕能力，但在忠实刻画复杂时序动态方面仍存在困难，开源模型显著落后。

Conclusion: 代码字幕是保真度更高的视频表示方式，其价值能否兑现取决于 MLLM 的忠实生成能力；当前模型在该能力上仍有明显缺口，尤其在时序动态建模方面。

Abstract: Video captioning aims to preserve visual information through compact descriptions, predominantly using natural language. However, precisely expressing fine-grained spatial structures and temporal dynamics in natural language often leads to lengthy and potentially ambiguous descriptions. In this work, we explore Code as Video Caption, a paradigm where executable programs serve as a complementary representation of visual content through graphical primitives, layout rules, and functions of time. To examine whether code captions effectively preserve video information, we compare them with natural language captions on downstream question answering and video reconstruction tasks. Our results show that code captions achieve superior reasoning accuracy and reconstruction fidelity. Despite these advantages, realizing the potential of code captioning depends on whether current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate faithful code captions. To systematically assess this capability, we introduce CodeCap-Bench, which comprises 323 videos spanning five categories of structured 2D content, along with fine-grained annotations and a multi-level evaluation framework. Extensive experiments reveal that while leading proprietary MLLMs demonstrate promising code captioning capabilities, they still struggle to faithfully capture complex temporal dynamics, with open-source models lagging substantially behind.

</details>


### [108] [TimeLineXR: An Interactive Exploration of Time in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37349)
*Nidham Tekaya,Markus Passecker,Matthias Zeppelzauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeLineXR 将 VLM 嵌入空间中隐含的时间轴转化为可交互、可探索的 3D/WebXR 场景，支持用户上传图像并查看其在时间轴上的落点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明 VLM 能在嵌入空间中以低维非线性流形编码时间，可定量复现，但该结构对终端用户不可见、不可查询、不可检视。作者希望让普通用户能直观看到并查询这一时间结构。

Method: 构建两个客户端（Web 演示与基于 Three.js 的 WebXR 房间级 3D 场景），共用同一预测流水线与缓存的时间索引：用 CLIP 编码查询图像，经余弦核 KPCA 投影，再吸附到 Bézier 时间轴上；WebXR 场景中的时间轴可抓取、缩放、检视，并通过投掷图像卡片进行查询。

Result: 每张图像被映射为时间轴上的一个点，用户可比较不同物体的落点，并读出其估计的"首次出现年份"；提供开源代码与复现步骤。

Conclusion: TimeLineXR 把 VLM 内在时间表征从定量分析结果转化为可视、可交互的探索工具，降低了该结构的使用门槛，并为时间表征的可解释性提供了交互式验证界面。

Abstract: Recently, it has been shown that vision-language models (VLMs) have a certain intrinsic understanding of time. They can estimate the temporal origin of objects and encode time along a low-dimensional, non-linear manifold inside their embedding space~\cite{tekaya2025matter}. That result is quantitative and reproducible, but the structure itself stays out of reach for end users: they cannot see it, query it with their own images, or inspect where a query lands relative to other years on the time axis. We present TimeLineXR, an interactive demonstration that turns this time axis into an explorable 3D scene. A web demo lets users upload or select images and see each placed on a color-coded 3D time axis; a Three.js WebXR scene renders the same time axis as a room-scale representation that can be grabbed, scaled, inspected, and queried by throwing image cards into it. Both clients run the same prediction pipeline against the same cached temporal index: encode the query with CLIP, project it through cosine-kernel KPCA, and snap it to the Bézier time axis. The system turns each image into a point on the time axis, so users can compare where different objects land and read off their estimated first year of appearance.Code source and reproduction steps can be found in: https://github.com/sterngefeuert/timeline-xr.

</details>


### [109] [CTBakeGS: training-free baking of photorealistic CT volume renderings into 3D Gaussian splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37406)
*Haruki Fukuda,Constantin Lux,Wolf Schweitzer,Tadashi Hosoya,Lars Ebert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的CT体渲染到3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）模型的直接转换方法，将泼溅点放置于毫米级自适应八叉树上，在GPU上按渲染器光照解析计算颜色与不透明度，并随机保留低于1/255不透明度下限的微弱泼溅，使普通电脑在约1分钟内生成可实时渲染的逼真CT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逼真的CT体渲染能直观呈现解剖结构，但依赖CT数据、专用软件与强算力GPU；3DGS可在较弱设备上实时显示逼真场景，然而已有医学方法需为每个场景训练数分钟至数小时，成本过高。因此作者希望跳过训练，直接把CT体渲染转换为3DGS模型。

Method: （1）在毫米级细胞的自适应八叉树上放置泼溅点；（2）在GPU上结合渲染器光照计算泼溅点的颜色与不透明度；（3）将细胞不透明度解析地转换为泼溅不透明度；（4）对低于标准3DGS光栅化器1/255不透明度下限的微弱泼溅按概率随机保留；（5）对6组公开CT扫描（1.5 mm体素）用三种传输函数转换，并以PSNR、结构相似性和学习感知指标与收敛参考对比。

Result: 200万泼溅点模型达到PSNR：骨骼32.6±0.9 dB、增强对比血管30.1±1.0 dB、皮肤与骨骼32.3±1.5 dB。单次转换（每个模型通常2–3次）在当前笔记本上耗时4–30 s，在配4 GB显卡的台式机上耗时9–60 s。笔记本上以800×800像素渲染达68 fps。

Conclusion: 免训练转换可在普通电脑上以每次转换约1分钟或更短时间，生成逼真CT渲染的实时3DGS模型，其保真度足以支持交互式阅片查看。

Abstract: Photorealistic volume rendering of computed tomography (CT) may convey anatomy intuitively, but it needs the CT data, dedicated software and a powerful graphics processor. Three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS) displays photorealistic scenes in real time on less powerful devices, but existing medical approaches train each scene for minutes to hours. We aimed to convert a CT volume rendering directly into a 3DGS model without training. Splats were placed on an adaptive octree of millimetre-sized cells. Their colour and opacity were computed on the graphics processor with the renderer's lighting, cell opacity was converted analytically into splat opacity, and faint splats were retained stochastically above the 1/255 opacity floor of standard 3DGS rasterisers. Six public CT scans (1.5-mm voxels) were converted with three transfer functions and compared with converged references by peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity and a learned perceptual metric. Two-million-splat models reached a PSNR of 32.6 +/- 0.9 dB for bone, 30.1 +/- 1.0 dB for contrast-enhanced vessels and 32.3 +/- 1.5 dB for skin and bone. A conversion pass (usually two or three per model) took 4-30 s on a current laptop and 9-60 s on a desktop with a 4-GB graphics card. On the laptop, the models rendered at 68 frames per second at 800 x 800 pixels. Training-free conversion produced real-time 3DGS models of photorealistic CT renderings in about a minute or less per conversion pass on ordinary computers, with fidelity that we consider sufficient for interactive viewing.

</details>


### [110] [Segmentation Confidence for Arbitrary CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37431)
*Baris Oguz,Xing Yao,Claudia Tawil,Alison Pouch,Brett Byram,Gabriel Arenas,Nadav Schwartz,Ipek Oguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文探索可即插即用于现有分割流程的体素级不确定性估计方法，并提出由不确定性图推导单一置信度评分的新方法，用于预测未见数据上的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在医学图像分割中广泛应用，但缺乏内在的置信度度量；现有不确定性估计方法往往需要定制网络结构、专门训练设置或从头训练，且从不确定性图汇总为单一置信分数并不直观。

Method: 研究无需从头训练的、可附加于现有分割流程的体素级不确定性估计方法；同时提出一种从不确定性图估计单一置信度评分的新方法。

Result: 在不同ROI与模态上的评估表明，所提出的置信度评分能够预测模型在未见数据上的分割性能。

Conclusion: 体素级不确定性估计可低成本集成到既有分割流程中，且汇总得到的单一置信度评分可作为分割质量的可靠预测指标。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) are widely used for medical image segmentation. However, they lack an inherent measure of confidence. Existing methods for estimating prediction uncertainty often require a custom network architecture and/or dedicated training setups and need to be trained from scratch. In addition, the deduction of a single confidence score per input from the uncertainty maps is not always straightforward. In this study, we explore voxel-based uncertainty estimation methods that can be added to existing segmentation pipelines without training from scratch. We also propose a novel method to estimate a single confidence score from the uncertainty maps. Our evaluations on different ROIs and modalities suggest that our confidence score can predict segmentation performance in unseen data.

</details>


### [111] [Representing the Unseen: Rethinking Visual Imagination for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37437)
*Zengqi Zhao,Zun Wang,Shoubin Yu,Mohit Bansal,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出ImagiRep基准，系统比较同一未观测状态的RGB与六种替代表示（深度、全景、俯视几何等）对空间推理的影响，发现准确的想象RGB并不保证准确的空间推理，不同问题偏好不同表示；进而提出轻量测试时自适应表示选择策略GEARS，性能优于现有空间想象方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型普遍以RGB视图作为想象未观测状态的默认接口，但RGB成为默认更多是因为便于视觉生成，而非对下游推理最有效。不同空间问题可能受益于同一想象状态的不同表示：俯视几何利于全局距离比较，全景视图为开放空间推理提供更广上下文。因此需要隔离"表示"在空间想象中的作用。

Method: 构建ImagiRep——基于仿真器的基准，含922个问题、7种问题类型、两种设置（Viewpoint Imagination与Cross-view Integration），对同一未观测状态同时提供oracle RGB及六种替代表示（如深度、全景视图、俯视几何），实现受控比较；并验证由世界模型估计状态导出的表示是否呈现类似趋势。在此基础上提出GEARS：一种轻量级测试时自适应表示选择策略。

Result: 受控比较显示：即使想象出的RGB准确，也不能保证空间推理准确；不同问题类型受益于不同表示。对世界模型估计状态导出的表示，趋势相似。值得注意的是，仅用估计RGB可能误导空间推理，而替代表示仍保留互补收益。GEARS优于现有空间想象方法。

Conclusion: 对未观测状态的推理不能仅依赖生成RGB视图；表示选择本身是关键变量。GEARS表明，通过测试时自适应选择表示可有效提升空间想象性能，提示未来世界模型应支持多表示接口而非单一RGB。

Abstract: Visual world models increasingly support spatial reasoning by generating RGB views of unobserved states. However, RGB has become the default interface largely because it is well suited for visual generation, rather than necessarily being the most effective representation for downstream reasoning. Different spatial questions may benefit from different representations of the same imagined state: top-down geometry can facilitate global distance comparisons, while panoramic views provide broader context for reasoning about open space. To isolate the role of representation in spatial imagination, we introduce ImagiRep, a simulator-grounded benchmark of 922 questions spanning seven question types across two settings, Viewpoint Imagination and Cross-view Integration, providing oracle RGB and six alternative representations (e.g., depth, panoramic views, and top-down geometry) of the same unobserved state. This controlled comparison reveals that even accurate imagined RGB does not guarantee accurate spatial reasoning, and different questions benefit from different representations. Similar trends hold for representations derived from world-model-estimated states. Notably, estimated RGB alone can mislead spatial reasoning, while alternative representations retain complementary benefits. Building on these findings, we introduce GEARS, a lightweight test-time strategy for adaptive representation selection, outperforming existing spatial imagination methods and showing that reasoning about unseen states requires more than generating RGB views.

</details>


### [112] [PerceptRL: Making Visual Perception Rewardable in MLLMs from Answers to Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37471)
*Tongkun Guan,Zhibo Yang,Mingkun Yang,Haocheng Wang,Wei Shen,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptRL在MLLM后训练的两个互补层面强化感知：一是基于多智能体流程从密集图像描述中抽取可验证错误并转为规则奖励QA的感知强化学习，二是借助多轮视觉工具检查、坐标注入与基于grounding的过程奖励的智能体化感知RL，在七个基准上平均准确率达71.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉感知是多模态大模型后训练阶段的瓶颈，感知错误会限制以推理为中心的优化收益。图像-描述预训练虽能改善感知，但后续阶段数据混合与目标变化会削弱其作用；而在后训练中持续进行描述监督又因描述主观、信息密集，难以设计可验证奖励。

Method: 方法分两个层次。感知RL：用多智能体流程从密集图像描述中抽取已验证错误，将对应视觉事实转化为固定答案标签、规则奖励的QA对；链式QA连接相关错误，多选QA组合不相关错误；设置面向高错误领域的专用路线（定制验证）与领域无关的通用路线。感知智能体化RL：通过多轮视觉工具检查处理单轮难以解决的细粒度、密集情形；针对工具使用模型中仍存在的坐标漂移问题，采用坐标注入纠正冷启动轨迹中的空间操作，并用grounding感知的过程奖励同时监督中间空间保真度与最终答案正确性。训练流程为共享冷启动SFT、单轮感知RL、多轮感知智能体化RL。

Result: 在统一工具使用协议下的七个基准上，PerceptRL平均准确率达71.6%，分别超过使用相同工具的基座模型15.7个百分点、超过所评估的最强前沿VLM 5.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 将描述级感知监督转化为可验证的规则奖励RL，并结合工具化多轮检查、坐标注入与过程奖励，能有效缓解MLLM后训练中的细粒度感知瓶颈，带来显著且跨基准一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception remains a bottleneck during post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as perceptual errors can constrain gains from reasoning-focused optimization. Although image-caption pre-training improves perception, its influence can weaken as subsequent stages change data mixtures and objectives. Continuing caption supervision during post-training is challenging because subjective, information-dense descriptions complicate verifiable reward design. We present PerceptRL, which strengthens perception at two complementary levels. Perception RL uses a multi-agent pipeline to extract verified errors from dense image captions and convert the corresponding visual facts into QA pairs with fixed answer labels and rule-based rewards. Chain QA links correlated errors, while multiple-choice QA combines unrelated errors. A specialized route targets high-error domains with tailored verification, and a domain-agnostic general route covers errors beyond these domains. Perception Agentic RL addresses fine-grained and dense cases unresolved by single-turn Perception RL through multi-turn inspection with visual tools. We observe coordinate drift even in models trained to use tools, where incorrect crops or annotations distort evidence and propagate errors. Coordinate Injection corrects spatial operations in cold-start trajectories, while a grounding-aware process reward supervises intermediate spatial fidelity alongside final-answer correctness. Training proceeds through shared cold-start SFT, single-turn Perception RL, and multi-turn Perception Agentic RL. Across seven benchmarks under a shared tool-use protocol, PerceptRL achieves 71.6% average accuracy, surpassing the base model with the same tools and the strongest evaluated frontier VLM by 15.7 and 5.2 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [113] [How Reliable Is Individualized Functional-Network Attribution in Resting-State fMRI Cognitive Prediction in ADNI?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37472)
*Sheethal Bhat,Mahfuzur Rahman Chowdhury,Imam Ahmed Shaik,Rose Dawn Bharath,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估rs-fMRI个体化网络消融归因的重测信度：分半信度近上限，但扫描-重扫描信度显著下降，且须用置换零假设区分个体特异成分与群体共享结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化归因（哪些功能网络驱动某受试者的模型预测）常被提议作为可解释的rs-fMRI生物标志物，但其重测信度很少被量化，个体特异性可能被高估。

Method: 在ADNI N=875名受试者上训练冻结的静息态功能连接预测器（Schaefer-200 FC预测MMSE/ADAS-Cog13），对每名受试者做网络消融归因；采用分半信度、扫描-重扫描信度，以及受试者水平置换零假设（含错配受试者零分布与匹配对检验）进行对照。

Result: 分半信度接近上限（Spearman ρ=0.948/0.950），扫描-重扫描时降至ρ=0.609/0.616；错配受试者零假设下相关仍达ρ≈0.46–0.47，显示强群体共享结构，匹配对显著高于该零分布（z≈6.6–6.9，p≤10^-4）。

Conclusion: ADNI中个体化网络归因在强群体共享模式之上仅含有限但非零的个体特异成分；建议同时报告扫描-重扫描信度与置换零假设对照，而非仅用同session代理指标，以避免夸大个体特异性。

Abstract: Individualized attribution estimates which functional networks drive a model prediction for a given subject and is often proposed as an interpretable resting-state fMRI (rs-fMRI) biomarker, but its test-retest reliability is rarely quantified. We evaluate per-subject network-ablation attribution for a frozen resting-state functional connectivity (FC) predictor (Schaefer-200 FC predicting MMSE/ADAS-Cog13) trained on N=875 subjects from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), using, and (iii) a subject-level permutation null. Split-half reliability is near-ceiling (Spearman p=0.948/0.950) but drops on scan-rescan (p=0.609/0.616). Under the mismatched-subject null, correlations remain substantial (p~0.46-0.47), indicating strong population-shared structure, yet matched pairs exceed this null (z~6.6-6.9, p<=10-4). Thus, in ADNI, individualized network attribution contains a modest but non-zero person-specific component atop a strong population-shared pattern. We recommend reporting scan-rescan reliability together with a permutation-null control, rather than within-session proxies alone, to avoid overstating individual specificity.

</details>


### [114] [ALADDIN: Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37503)
*Ivan Shchekotov,Timofei Gritsaev,Viacheslav Pirogov,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALADDIN 通过对抗式蒸馏将预训练多步流匹配模型压缩为一步人脸交换生成器，在真实性、目标属性保持上达到一步法 SOTA，并以 28 倍更少网络评估匹配最佳多步模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式人脸交换真实感强但需多次网络评估，速度慢；GAN 从零训练仅依赖对抗信号学真实感，效果明显较弱。需要在保持高质量的同时实现高效的一步推理。

Method: 从预训练多步 flow-matching 模型初始化生成器，在身份与目标保持约束下进行对抗训练；并用三元组伪标签监督训练学生模型，以暴露身份迁移与目标属性保持之间的可控权衡。

Result: 在可比身份迁移水平下，ALADDIN 在一步法中于真实感与目标保持方面为 SOTA；匹配最佳多步模型的同时网络评估次数减少 28 倍、训练对少一个数量级。此外指出标准评测协议偏向使用同一身份编码器训练与打分的方法，并用独立编码器重新评估全部基线。

Conclusion: 对抗蒸馏是从多步流匹配模型获得高效一步人脸交换的有效路线；同时揭示了评测协议中的编码器偏差问题，需用独立编码器重评以保证公平比较。

Abstract: Face swapping transfers a source identity onto a target image while preserving the target's pose, expression, hair, and lighting. The most realistic current methods are diffusion-based, but they need many network evaluations per image. GAN-based methods are fast, yet trained from scratch they must learn realism from the adversarial signal alone and produce visibly weaker results. We present ALADDIN (Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping), a one-step face swapper obtained by initialising the generator from a pre-trained multi-step flow-matching model and training it adversarially under identity and target-preservation constraints. We expose a controllable trade-off between identity transfer and target-attribute preservation by training a student model with triplet pseudo-label supervision. We additionally identify a bias in the standard evaluation protocol, which favours methods trained against the same identity encoder used to score them, and re-evaluate all baselines with an independent one. ALADDIN is state-of-the-art among one-step methods in realism and target preservation at comparable identity transfer, and matches the best multi-step model with 28 times fewer network evaluations and an order of magnitude fewer training pairs.

</details>


### [115] [A note on data-efficient finetuning of vision foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37510)
*Niclas Hergenröther,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对注意力、卷积和Mamba三类基础模型在不同迁移范式（视觉提示VP、线性探测LP、全量微调FFT、LoRA）下的表现进行系统性实证评估，扩展模型与技术选择并调优超参数，同时探索正则化技术以提升VP性能，为资源受限下的高效微调提供实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Mamba等新基础模型家族涌现以及AutoVP等视觉提示流程趋于成熟，现有方法缺乏对新模型与多种微调范式的系统比较，难以理解资源受限条件下高效微调的进展、挑战与机会。

Method: 构建实证综述式基准：交叉比较三类基础模型家族（基于注意力、卷积、Mamba）与四种迁移范式（VP、LP、FFT、LoRA），扩大评估模型和技术范围并对超参数进行调优；进一步将常用正则化技术应用于VP以提升其性能。

Result: 给出基础模型家族与迁移范式交互关系的简明实证概览，揭示不同组合的性能表现，并展示正则化技术在VP场景下对性能的增益。

Conclusion: 为资源受限条件下的高效微调提供系统性经验参考与面向用户的实践指南，明确该领域的进展与待解挑战。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models to a new task within or beyond the training domain is the de facto standard for building specialized machine learning models. Recently, Visual Prompting (VP) has emerged as a promising technique for efficient knowledge transfer. As new foundation model families~(like Mamba) get introduced and VP pipelines such as AutoVP reach greater maturity, we find a growing need for a systematic evaluation of current approaches. In this work, we assess the performance of the latest models, comparing them to earlier architectures and alternative fine-tuning methods, to better understand the progress, challenges and opportunities in the field of efficient fine-tuning under resource limitations. Towards this goal, this paper provides a concise empirical overview of the interactions among foundation model families (Attention-, Convolution-, and Mamba-based) and transfer paradigms: VP, Linear Probing (LP), Full Fine-tuning (FFT) and Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Our work builds up on previous findings by broadening the selection of evaluated models and techniques while tuning hyperparameters. In the interest of delivering practical guidelines for the user, we also explore application of prevalent regularization techniques to boost performance in the context of VP.

</details>


### [116] [GroundControl: Satellite-Conditioned Multi-View Diffusion for Reconstruction-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37545)
*Akshay Krishnan,Matias Turkulainen,Jamie Wynn,Zawar Qureshi,Mohamed Sayed,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Sara Vicente,James Hays,Michael Firman,Daniyar Turmukhambetov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以卫星图像作为额外条件信号来约束多视角扩散模型，从而缓解室外场景外推时空间布局幻觉的问题，并保留预训练多视角先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角/视频扩散模型在稀疏图像新视角合成中画质高，但当目标视角超出输入视角（场景外推）时容易幻觉出不真实的空间布局。作者选择室外场景，并假设卫星图像可提供可靠的全局场景布局信息来弥补街景输入的空间覆盖不足。

Method: 构建卫星条件化的多视角扩散模型：以卫星图像与街景输入图像共同作为条件生成新视角；采用基于交叉注意力的条件注入方式，避免改动主干权重，从而保留预训练的多视角扩散先验；不使用三维场景重建，仅依赖带位姿的街景与卫星视角。

Result: 在室外数据集上的实验表明，该方法在新视角合成上优于仅卫星条件与仅街景条件的基线，在目标视角与源视角重叠极少的困难场景中优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 卫星图像可作为有效的全局布局先验，通过交叉注意力条件化与预训练多视角扩散先验结合，可在不依赖三维重建的情况下显著提升室外新视角合成在外推情形下的空间一致性与真实感。

Abstract: Multi-view or video diffusion models have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for novel-view synthesis from sparse image collections. While they can produce high quality images, they tend to hallucinate unrealistic spatial layouts when synthesizing views outside the input views (scene extrapolation). We propose to address this challenge for outdoor scenes by using satellite images as an additional information source to condition the multi-view generative model. We introduce a new satellite-conditioned multi-view diffusion model that combines conditioning from a satellite image and street-level input images when generating novel-views. Our cross-attention based conditioning allows us to retain pretrained multi-view diffusion priors to generate seamless, realistic, novel views from a handful of source views, while also adhering to the scene layout as seen from satellite images. Our model does not use 3D scene reconstruction, and only relies on posed street and satellite views. Our experiments on outdoor datasets show that our model is better than both satellite-conditioned or street-view conditioned baselines on novel-view synthesis, especially on challenging cases where target views have very little overlap with source views. Project page: https://nianticspatial.github.io/groundcontrol

</details>


### [117] [ReferStream: Interactive Subject Injection for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37566)
*Shuolin Xu,Guanyuan Li,Boyu Li,Kelu Yao,Xiaogang Xu,Hongchuan Yu,Shuicheng Yan,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的 ReferStream 框架，通过联合潜变量与缓存恢复实现在流式视频生成中自然注入新主体，并构建 ReferStream Bench 基准；在文本一致性、主体相似度与计数准确率上优于含离线方法的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成中新主体注入需兼顾新参考信息融合与已建立主体/场景的保持：独立于当前场景编码参考缺乏整合引导，而长期驻留记忆特征又会干扰已建立的上下文。

Method: 无需额外训练，结合基于当前场景的参考构建与瞬态缓存注入：Joint Latent 将参考潜变量放入最新场景潜变量的目标区域，使参考特征在上下文中被提取；Cache Restoration 用这些特征条件化注入块，随后恢复被覆盖的全局缓存条目并保留新生成的历史，从而在不重训生成器、不回改已输出帧的前提下引入新视觉条件。

Result: 在 ReferStream Bench（含预定参考到达、演化指令及主体存在标注，覆盖 InShot 与 MultiShot）上，文本一致性、主体相似度与计数准确率均高于对比基线，包括提前获得全部参考的离线方法。

Conclusion: 瞬态参考注入可在流式生成中实现新主体的高保真融入与既有主体保持，且无需训练；新基准为事件调度驱动的流式参考注入评估提供了统一协议。

Abstract: Injecting new subjects naturally during streaming video generation requires integrating newly supplied visual references into the existing video while preserving established subjects and the scene. Encoding references independently of the current scene provides little guidance for this integration, while keeping their features in persistent memory can interfere with established context. We propose ReferStream, a framework that requires no additional training and combines reference construction using the current scene with transient cache injection. Joint Latent places reference latents in target regions of the latest scene latent, allowing reference features to be extracted in context. Cache Restoration uses these features to condition the injection chunk, then restores the overwritten global cache entries while retaining the newly generated history. This procedure incorporates new visual conditions without retraining the generator or revising emitted frames. We further introduce ReferStream Bench, a new benchmark with scheduled reference arrivals, evolving instructions, and explicit annotations of subject presence for InShot and MultiShot generation. Evaluation follows the event schedule and measures both the fidelity of new subjects and the preservation of established subjects, alongside video quality, text adherence, and count accuracy. Experiments on ReferStream Bench show higher scores for text adherence, subject similarity, and count accuracy than the compared baselines, including offline methods given all references in advance. Project page: https://xushuolin.github.io/ReferStream/.

</details>


### [118] [The Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576)
*Xuran Hu,Yuru Jia,Tengxi Wang,Miloš Daković,Ljubiša Stanković,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 p-Bridge：一个以指数 p 与累积测量 ν 为坐标的 Tweedie 噪声统一扩散去噪框架，用随机桥把去噪解读为逐步测量累积，并以单一指数条件网络适配高斯/泊松/Gamma 等多种信号相关噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散去噪多假设高斯扰动，而真实传感器噪声服从信号相关（方差—强度幂律）统计，Gaussian/Poisson/Gamma 等模型各自为政，缺乏统一且可在指数间泛化的去噪框架。

Method: 以噪声指数 p（方差—强度关系）与累积测量 ν（噪声水平）构成"噪声平面"，统一 Gaussian、Poisson、Gamma 模型；用独立增量随机桥连接不同测量分布，允许指定起点与停止指数，实现从观测测量水平恢复到指定更高水平；测量叠加得到共享反向均值更新，配以 deviance 目标学习条件均值；构建潜变量复合 Poisson–Gamma 构造以支持中间指数（非整数 p）采样；训练单一 p 条件化网络。

Result: 指数条件化相较固定指数模型提升跨指数鲁棒性；在使用估计指数时，共享网络性能与 oracle 选择的五个专家模型集合相当；在合成图像、FMD 荧光显微与多传感器 SAR 图像上均验证了对不同信号相关噪声机制的有效性。

Conclusion: p-Bridge 为 Tweedie 族噪声提供了统一、可插值的扩散去噪范式，去噪具有测量累积的物理含义，且单一条件网络可覆盖多噪声指数，具备跨噪声机制的实用鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based denoising commonly uses Gaussian perturbations, whereas sensor noise follows diverse signal-dependent statistical laws. In this paper, we propose $p$-Bridge, a unified measurement-indexed diffusion framework for Tweedie noise. The noise exponent $p$ describes the variance--intensity relation, while accumulated measurement $ν$ controls the noise level. Together, they define a noise plane that includes Gaussian, Poisson, and Gamma models. The stochastic bridge connects measurement distributions through independent increments, giving denoising a physical interpretation as progressive measurement accumulation. Both the starting and stopping indices can be specified, allowing restoration from an observed measurement level to a prescribed higher level. Measurement superposition yields a shared reverse-mean update, paired with a deviance-based objective for learning the required conditional mean. A latent compound Poisson--Gamma construction enables sampling at intermediate exponents. A single exponent-conditioned network adapts to different Tweedie noise models without separate training for each exponent. Experiments show that exponent conditioning improves cross-exponent robustness over fixed-exponent models. Using estimated exponents, the shared network achieves robustness comparable to an oracle-selected set of five specialist models. Experiments on synthetic images, FMD fluorescence microscopy, and multi-sensor SAR images further demonstrate its effectiveness across different signal-dependent noise regimes.

</details>


### [119] [As the Story Unfolds: Watching a Film and Identifying Characters as a Human Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37579)
*Zhongrui Gui,Junyu Xie,Tengda Han,Weidi Xie,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"角色命名"任务：仅凭影片自身的对话人名与视频上下文，识别主要角色并为其分配姓名，无需外部演员表或预建角色库，并构建数据集、视频-对话模型与渐进式角色库框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影中角色是随剧情逐步出场的，而现有方法通常依赖外部演员表、演员照片或预建角色库，违背了"像人一样在线理解电影"的设定。作者希望直接从影片本身推断角色身份。

Method: 形式化定义角色命名任务（给定对话中出现的人名与该处视频上下文，判定画面中哪个角色对应此名）；构建数据集与标注，用于评测角色命名和角色首次出场；设计视频-对话联合建模模型；提出随影片推进逐步构建角色库的渐进式框架；并与商用多模态大模型对比。

Result: 提供了可评测角色命名与首次出场的基准数据，所提视频-对话模型在该任务上与专有多模态大模型进行了对比（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 该工作支持在线、类人的电影理解，即角色身份完全从影片内部线索推断，无需外部监督信息。

Abstract: Films introduce characters incrementally as they progress. We study the problem of identifying principal characters and assigning their names directly from the movie itself, without using external cast lists, actor photographs, or pre-built character banks. We introduce the character naming task: given a name mentioned in dialogue and the associated video context, the model must determine which visible character bears that name. This task is challenging because name resolution often depends on cinematic cues such as shot-reverse-shot structure, self-introduction, direct address, third-party reference, gaze, and temporal continuity. We provide a dataset and annotations for evaluating character naming and first character appearances, develop a video-and-dialogue model for the task, and compare it with proprietary multimodal large language models. We further introduce a progressive framework for building a character bank as the film unfolds. Together, these contributions support online, human-like movie understanding in which character identities are inferred from the film itself.

</details>


### [120] [Zero-Shot Multimodal Event Spotting in Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37583)
*Sadia Kamal,Mahmudul Hasan,Tony Braskich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零样本足球事件检测框架：用故事板+解说文本，由通用多模态模型联合处理，在3场比赛130个事件上取得72.0% F1@5s，跨数据集优于SoccerNet训练的CALF和NetVLAD++，推理成本降低72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球事件检测方法依赖大规模标注数据和足球专用训练，泛化性与成本受限。

Method: 将整场比赛切分为重叠片段，每片段用紧凑视觉故事板与对齐解说文本表示，交由通用多模态模型联合推理，实现事件的检测、分类与时间定位，无需任务特定参数训练；并引入基于证据的精彩度打分阶段。

Result: 在3场人工标注、共130个事件的比赛上，最佳配置达72.0% F1@5s；跨数据集设置下优于SoccerNet训练的CALF与NetVLAD++；故事板表示相较逐帧输入降低72%推理成本。另分析了模型族、输入表示与提示优化的影响。

Conclusion: 零样本、免训练的多模态框架可有效完成足球事件检测与定位，兼顾跨域泛化与效率，并通过自然语言解释支持集锦选取。

Abstract: Automatic soccer event spotting supports highlight generation, video indexing, and sports analytics, but existing methods depend on large annotated datasets and soccer-specific training. We present a zero-shot framework that detects, classifies, and temporally localizes events in full-length soccer broadcasts without task-specific parameter training. Each match is segmented into overlapping clips represented by a compact visual storyboard and the aligned commentary transcript, which a general-purpose multimodal model processes jointly. On three manually annotated matches containing 130 events, the best configuration achieves 72.0% F1@5s and outperforms SoccerNet-trained CALF and NetVLAD++ in a cross-dataset setting, while the storyboard representation reduces inference cost by 72% compared with individual frames. We further analyze the effects of model family, input representation, and prompt optimization, and introduce an evidence-grounded excitement-scoring stage that ranks detected events with natural-language explanations for highlight selection.

</details>


### [121] [MoI-Bench: A Benchmark for Image Generation and Editing on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37599)
*Kailai Feng,Haoxiang Rao,Yuxiang Wei,Bo Chen,Junsong Li,Yang Pan,Chengkun Cai,Jagmohan Chauhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoI-Bench：基于移动用户需求的设备端图像编辑基准（64子任务、1150样本），以操作×目标×评价准则解耦分析能力差距，并用质量-可部署性二维图指导部署选型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端图像模型需兼顾用户需求、任务类型与资源约束，但现有基准难以揭示设备端与服务器端模型的细粒度能力差距，缺乏对改进方向的具体指导。

Method: 构建覆盖16种操作（如移除、替换）与8类视觉目标（如人像、文字）两个正交轴、组合形成64子任务与1150样本的基准；提出MoI-Eval，含任务特定准则（指令遵循、视觉质量、细节保持）、样本级评测问题，并沿操作/目标/准则轴聚合为整体质量与能力画像；评测代表性设备端与服务器端模型并测量移动端部署成本。

Result: 解耦的“操作×目标”设计能提供更细粒度指导，定位如“文字替换”等具体能力短板而非笼统应用目标；质量-可部署性二维图显示设备端模型已能高效生成或编辑，扩展任务覆盖比继续优化部署效率更迫切。

Conclusion: 后续进展需提升编辑质量（尤其身份保持与文字渲染）并拓宽任务覆盖；基准从单分数排名转向二维能力图谱，更贴合移动端实际应用评估。

Abstract: Developing on-device image models requires considering practical user needs, task types and device resource constraints. However, existing benchmarks provide limited guidance on identifying capability gaps between on-device models and server-side models. We introduce MoI-Bench, a benchmark grounded in mobile user needs, comprising 64 subtasks and 1150 samples. It defines 16 operations, such as removal and replacement, and eight visual target categories covering content such as portraits and text. These form two orthogonal axes whose applicable combinations define specific tasks, such as removing facial makeup. To better guide model improvement, we further propose MoI-Eval, which introduces task-specific criteria covering instruction following, visual quality and detail preservation. We further construct sample-level evaluation questions and aggregate their scores into overall quality and capability profiles along the operation, target and criteria axes. We evaluate representative on-device and server-side models and measure mobile deployment costs. Results show that this decoupled Operation x Target design provides finer-grained guidance for improving compact models. For example, it highlights the need to prioritize specific capability gaps (e.g., text-replacement) over broad application-level goals (e.g., improving text editing). Unlike conventional benchmarks that rank models using a single score, we introduce a two-dimensional Quality-Deployability capability map to assess models for practical mobile use. The map shows that current on-device models can achieve efficient generation or editing but expanding their task coverage deserves greater attention than further improving deployment efficiency. Further progress requires improving editing quality, particularly identity preservation and text rendering, while broadening task coverage.

</details>


### [122] [VideoLVR: Latent Visual Reasoning over Grounded Video Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37613)
*Shilong Li,Xingyuan Bu,Hao Shi,Yun Xing,Weixin Feng,Qiangpeng Yang,Shilei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoLVR 用局部视频区域构建"潜在视觉证据"并插入推理链，以视觉 token 预测目标统一重建与文本生成，可在不显著扩展上下文的前提下缓解视觉遗忘、提升细粒度视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言的思维链推理难以让模型可靠地回看相关视觉证据，导致"视觉遗忘"，削弱对视频内容的推理能力。现有两类做法各有缺陷：文本坐标只给符号化位置而非视觉内容本身；重新插入裁剪区域或整帧虽保留视觉细节，却造成上下文大幅膨胀。

Method: 1) 在相关视频时刻从局部区域构建潜在视觉证据，为中间推理步骤提供视觉支撑；2) 提出视觉 token 预测目标，将视觉 token 重建表述为对由输入视频派生的词表进行预测，从而把视觉证据重建与文本生成统一到同一 token 预测框架；3) 该共享框架使 MLLM 能在文本 token 与潜在视觉状态交织的序列上推理，并整合不同时刻的证据；4) 进一步扩展到强化学习，把采样得到的视觉 token 与文本 token 一并视为动作，以最终答案奖励联合优化策略。5) 通过高效回访任务相关视觉信息，避免上下文显著扩展。

Result: 在多个视频基准上验证了有效性，尤其在需要细粒度视觉证据的任务上提升明显。

Conclusion: 将视觉证据以潜在 token 形式嵌入推理链，并与文本生成共享预测框架（含 RL 联合优化），是兼顾视觉保真与上下文效率的可行方案，可有效缓解视觉遗忘并增强视频理解。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning has substantially advanced video understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, language-based reasoning traces do not provide a reliable mechanism for models to revisit relevant visual evidence, leading to visual forgetting and impairing their ability to reason over video content. Existing approaches maintain visual grounding during reasoning in two main ways: they either refer to evidence using textual coordinates, which specify symbolic locations rather than the visual content itself, or reinsert cropped regions or frames into the multimodal context, preserving visual details at the cost of substantial context expansion. To address these limitations, we introduce VideoLVR, which constructs latent visual evidence from local regions at relevant video moments, thereby providing visual support for intermediate reasoning steps. To supervise latent visual evidence generation, we introduce a visual token prediction objective that formulates visual token reconstruction as prediction over a vocabulary derived from the input video, thereby unifying visual evidence reconstruction and text generation within the same token prediction framework. This shared prediction framework enables MLLMs to reason over interleaved text tokens and latent visual states while integrating evidence from different moments. We further extend VideoLVR to reinforcement learning by treating sampled visual tokens as actions alongside text tokens and jointly optimizing the policy using final-answer rewards. By efficiently revisiting task-relevant visual information during reasoning, VideoLVR improves video understanding without substantially expanding the multimodal context. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness, especially on tasks requiring fine-grained visual evidence.

</details>


### [123] [Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619)
*Tudor Braicu,Joseph DelPreto,Jared Boyer,Mónika Farsang,Maximilian Stölzle,Tim Niklas Seyde,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建了同步自中心与五个异中心视频的网球数据集，评测基础视觉模型在七项时空预测任务上的能力，并进一步提出条件扩散模型由自中心视频实时生成全身网球运动学动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷机器人需要时空智能来理解场景、推理交互并预判未来事件，但基础视觉模型（尤其是以移动自中心视角时）能否支撑这些能力尚不清楚。论文旨在系统评估基础视觉模型在机器人自中心视角下的实时动态感知与预测能力。

Method: 采集并标注网球数据集：自中心视频与5路异中心视频同步，含估计的全局位置、接触时刻标注与击球类型标注。以自中心图像为输入，定义7项预测任务：2D自我球员与3D球定位、未来球员与球的轨迹、正手/反手击球预判、距下次自我球员触球的时间、以及该触球时刻的3D球位置。比较图像编码器、视频编码器与视觉语言模型搭配联合训练的预测头；并额外测试辅助深度信息与更长图像历史的影响。最后提出条件扩散模型，将视觉编码器预测转换为全身运动学网球动作，并在新预测到来时自适应调整。

Result: 最优模型达到：当前帧定位误差自我球员0.14 m、球0.54 m；1.1 s内未来预测误差自我球员0.34 m、球0.91 m；平均接触时间预测误差0.14 s。辅助深度信息显著提升3D球定位，说明度量深度推断仍是难点；更长的图像历史未改善球预测，表明表征更多利用场景上下文而非仅依赖帧间运动。

Conclusion: 基础视觉模型可为自中心视频下的实时动态机器人提供有效支撑，但度量深度与长时运动建模仍存在局限；结合条件扩散生成可实时产生可随预测更新而调整的全身动作，为机器人时空智能研究提供了基准与方向。

Abstract: Agile robots require spatiotemporal intelligence to interpret scenes, reason about interactions, and anticipate future events. Yet it remains unclear how well foundation vision models support these capabilities, particularly from a moving egocentric camera, which is a natural view for robots. To investigate this, we collect an annotated tennis dataset and train a suite of vision models on spatiotemporal tasks. Our dataset contains egocentric video synchronized with five allocentric videos, estimated global positions, labeled contact times, and annotated stroke types. We then evaluate seven prediction tasks from egocentric images: 2D ego player and 3D ball localization, future player and ball trajectories, forehand or backhand stroke anticipation, time to the next ego player ball contact, and 3D ball position at that contact. We compare image encoders, video encoders, and vision-language models with jointly trained prediction heads. The best performing models achieve mean current-frame localization errors of 0.14 m for the ego player and 0.54 m for the ball, future forecasting errors of 0.34 m for the ego player and 0.91 m for the ball over 1.1 s, and a mean contact-time prediction error of 0.14 s. Auxiliary depth information substantially improves 3D ball localization, suggesting that inferring metric depth remains a challenge. Longer image histories do not improve ball forecasting, indicating that representations exploit scene context rather than rely solely on inter-frame motion. Finally, we propose a conditional diffusion model to generate whole-body kinematic tennis motion from vision encoder predictions on egocentric video at real-time rates, adapting movements as predictions are refined. These results move towards a deeper understanding of how foundation vision models can support real-time dynamic robots using egocentric video.

</details>


### [124] [Know, then Draw: A New Learning Paradigm for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37626)
*Yucheng Mao,Chongjian Ge,Zhuowen Tu,Hao Tan,Hanwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"自回归预训练获取知识 + 扩散后训练实现高保真渲染"的两阶段范式，系统研究目标函数、表征与Scaling规律，并以纯语义Tokenizer和6,600 H100小时训练3B文生图模型，在GenEval与DPG-Bench上取得86/87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成同时需要高层语义知识与细粒度像素细节，单一模型目标难以兼顾；作者希望明确划分"知识获取"与"高保真渲染"两类角色，并厘清预训练目标、表征形式与规模规律对下游渲染的影响。

Method: 1）将自回归预训练用于知识获取、扩散后训练用于高保真渲染；2）在受控条件下对比自回归与像素扩散作为预训练目标；3）分析AR单独生成质量与最终性能的相关性，指出语义质量更关键；4）据此设计仅用语言监督训练、无图像解码器、无重建损失的纯语义Tokenizer；5）拟合知识获取与渲染的计算最优Scaling law，比较AR预训练与从零训练的像素扩散模型的渐近渲染损失。

Result: 自回归是更强的下游渲染预训练目标，提升学习效率与最终生成质量；AR单独生成质量与最终性能并非必然相关，语义质量更重要；AR预训练相比从零训练的像素扩散模型降低了拟合的渐近渲染损失；按Scaling law指导，仅用6,600 H100 GPU小时训练3B参数文生图模型，GenEval得86、DPG-Bench得87。

Conclusion: 验证了"自回归知识预训练+扩散渲染后训练"范式在规模化下的有效性，纯语义Tokenizer与计算最优Scaling law为高效高保真视觉生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Visual generation requires both high-level semantic knowledge and fine-grained pixel details. We propose a learning paradigm that assigns these roles to autoregressive pretraining for knowledge acquisition and diffusion post-training for high-fidelity rendering. Within this framework, we study the objective, representation, and scaling of knowledge acquisition and rendering. Controlled comparisons identify autoregression as the stronger pretraining objective for downstream rendering, improving both learning efficiency and final generation quality. We further find that AR-only generation quality does not necessarily correlate with final performance; semantic quality matters more. This motivates a purely semantic tokenizer trained with language supervision alone, without an image decoder or reconstruction loss. We establish compute-optimal scaling laws for knowledge acquisition and rendering, and find that autoregressive pretraining lowers the fitted asymptotic rendering loss compared with pixel diffusion models trained from scratch. Guided by the laws, we train a 3B-parameter text-to-image model with only 6,600 H100 GPU-hours, achieving 86 on GenEval and 87 on DPG-Bench and validating the proposed paradigm at scale.

</details>


### [125] [Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Cross-View Temporal Object Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37633)
*Siyoon Jin,Dahyun Chung,Honggyu An,Sangbeom Lim,Wonjin Nam,Jaewoo Jung,WonJun Moon,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨视角时序物体对应任务，并给出统一框架XTrack：复用预训练视频DiT，以RGB到掩码的潜空间传输统一建模跨视角匹配与时序跟踪，配合聚焦跨视角注意力对齐、难负样本条件化、局部上下文裁剪及稠密伪标签流程，实现稳健的身份保持与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角对应方法多基于逐帧匹配或序列跟踪，在外观变化或遮挡下会累积、传播对应错误；且该任务缺乏稠密标注，难以进行视频级训练。因此需要同时保证跨视角与跨时间的物体身份一致性。

Method: 提出XTrack统一框架：将预训练视频DiT改造为RGB-to-mask潜空间传输，联合建模跨视角匹配与时序跟踪；引入focal cross-view attention alignment、hard negative conditioning与local context crop策略以保证可靠的跨视角定位；并设计伪标签策管流水线，将稀疏Ego-Exo4D标注转为稠密监督以支持视频级训练。

Result: 结果表明XTrack作为统一框架有效，具备稳健的时序一致性并保持良好的物体身份保持能力。

Conclusion: 跨视角时序物体对应可通过统一生成式传输框架解决；视频DiT的语义与时空先验可被复用，辅以注意力对齐、难负样本与局部裁剪等设计及稠密伪标签，可显著提升跨视角与跨时间的对应可靠性。

Abstract: Cross-view object correspondence identifies the same object across views. However, existing methods often rely on frame-wise matching or sequential tracking, which can propagate correspondence errors under appearance changes or occlusions. Therefore, we introduce cross-view temporal object correspondence, which requires preserving object identity across both views and time from a query mask. To address this, we propose XTrack, a unified framework which repurposes a pretrained video DiT with semantic and spatiotemporal priors for RGB-to-mask latent transport, jointly modeling cross-view matching and temporal tracking. However, naive video DiTs do not guarantee accurate cross-view grounding and often attend to semantically similar yet incorrect objects across views. Thus, we introduce focal cross-view attention alignment, hard negative conditioning, and local context crop strategies to ensure reliable grounding. Finally, beyond these architectural enhancements, we also tackle the lack of dense annotation by introducing a pseudo-label curation pipeline that converts sparse Ego-Exo4D annotations into dense supervision to enable effective video-level training. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of XTrack as a unified framework, ensuring robust temporal consistency and identity preservation.

</details>


### [126] [Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662)
*Jialv Zou,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Xu Huang,Yaxin Luo,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文重新审视"量化误差必然导致语义损失"的假设，发现有效比特预算才是离散视觉token下游性能的关键决定因素，并提出因子化向量量化（FVQ）以缓解码本饱和，使离散表示在多模态理解上匹配连续表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散视觉token被视为原生多模态建模的接口，但普遍观点认为量化误差造成不可逆的语义损失，使离散表示存在语义保真度的内在上限、连续表示被当作不可超越的上界。作者希望验证这一假设是否成立。

Method: 以多模态理解作为语义保真度的代理指标，考察量化误差与理解性能的关系；据此提出FVQ：对视觉特征的互补、固定维度视图用对称并行码本进行量化，使量化器在更大比特预算下仍持续获益。

Result: 量化误差与理解性能不可靠相关（误差更大的离散表示反而理解更好），有效比特预算是关键；现有量化器随码本增加趋于饱和。FVQ在更大比特预算下持续提升，最终在多模态理解上匹配连续表示；基于工业级多模态数据训练，在九项指标平均上比UniAR、Emu3-Chat分别高22.8%和59.2%，并首次在细粒度OCR等任务上与Qwen3-VL等强连续表示模型具竞争力。

Conclusion: 离散视觉表示可在大比特预算下达到与连续表示相当的多模态理解性能；作者总结了实现该竞争力的关键原则与实践配方，支持离散视觉表示作为大规模原生多模态基础模型基础的潜力，并开源代码与模型。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens represent vision in a discrete form analogous to language, offering a native interface for multimodal modeling under a shared next-token prediction objective. However, quantization error is widely assumed to cause unavoidable semantic loss, fundamentally limiting the semantic fidelity of discrete visual tokens and positioning continuous visual representations as an intrinsic upper bound. Using multimodal understanding as a proxy for semantic fidelity, we revisit this assumption and find quantization error unreliable, as discrete representations with substantially larger errors can achieve better understanding. Instead, the effective bit budget of discrete indices emerges as a key determinant of downstream performance. However, existing quantizers eventually saturate as additional codebooks contribute progressively less semantic information. As one approach to alleviating this saturation, we introduce Factorized Vector Quantization, which quantizes complementary, fixed-dimensional views of the visual feature with symmetric parallel codebooks. It continues to improve at larger bit budgets, ultimately matching continuous visual representations in multimodal understanding. Trained on industrial-scale multimodal data, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance among discrete-representation models, outperforming UniAR and Emu3-Chat by 22.8% and 59.2%, respectively, on a nine-metric average. To our knowledge, it is also the first discrete-representation model competitive with strong continuous-representation models such as Qwen3-VL, including on fine-grained OCR tasks. We further identify the key principles and a practical recipe enabling this competitiveness. These results and insights support the potential of discrete visual representations to underpin large-scale native multimodal foundation models. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/FVQ

</details>


### [127] [Honeycomb-Surfels: Compact Hexagonal Primitives for Efficient Scene Rendering and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37668)
*Prakhar Kaushik,Soumava Paul,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出六边形平面基元 Hexels，用一阶空间颜色场表示局部平面区域，在更低显存下显著超越 2DGS 的重建与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场与 splatting 虽推动了新视角合成与三维重建，但其显存与算力开销限制了在资源受限场景中的应用，需更高效的基元表示。

Method: 设计平面六边形表面元素（Hexels），利用其面积/边界高效的六边形支撑域与每个基元内的一阶（线性）空间颜色场，以更少、更大的基元拟合局部平面且外观平滑变化的区域。

Result: 在 34 个场景上，PSNR 较同显存的 2DGS 提升 4.53–7.38 dB 且训练显存更低；单基元对比最高提升 4.74 dB、显存更低且 FPS 快 2.31 倍；15 个 DTU 扫描误差降低 28.9%；6 个 Tanks and Temples 场景平均 F1 达 34.38%（2DGS 为 31.72%）；2D 图像表示亦优于 Image-GS。

Conclusion: Hexels 作为通用基元，在质量-效率权衡上推进了 Pareto 前沿。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction have advanced rapidly with radiance fields and splatting, yet their memory and computational demands limit their use in resource-constrained settings. We introduce Hexels: novel planar hexagonal surface elements with a first-order spatial color field for efficient 3D scene and 2D image representation. Hexels exploit locally planar regions with smoothly varying appearance using fewer, larger primitives. Their hexagonal support provides an area- and boundary-efficient domain, while the local color field captures appearance variation within each primitive. Across 34 scenes, Hexels improve PSNR over memory-matched 2DGS by 4.53-7.38 dB across datasets while requiring lower training VRAM. We further ablate Hexels by showing that per primitive, Hexels outperform 2DGS by upto 4.74 dB while using lower training VRAM and having 2.31 times faster FPS. Even in unconstrained settings, Hexels offers complementary quality-memory trade-offs. For efficient 3D surface reconstruction, Hexels reduce error by 28.9% compared with memory-matched 2DGS across 15 DTU scans. On 6 Tanks and Temples surface scenes, Hexels achieves the highest mean F1 among compared methods (34.38%, versus 31.72% for 2DGS). We further also show that Hexels, as a general primitive, improves 2D image representation over Image-GS. Hexels push the Pareto frontier on the quality-efficiency trade-off of surface-aligned primitives.

</details>
